# dev-loop archive

> Archived SEALED nodes moved out of `dev-loop.prime-mermaid.md` to keep
> the active diagram small (every worker session re-reads the active file
> in full). **Nothing deleted, nothing reworded** — every row below is
> copied verbatim from the active file. Only reason to open this file:
> looking up the full story behind an old node (the active file keeps a
> one-line pointer to it). `pick_next`/`verify_seal` never need to read
> this file — only non-archived nodes matter for picking/verifying work.
>
> Archived: 2026-08-29 (token-cost cleanup, operator-requested). Covers
> every SEALED node dated 2026-08-20 through 2026-08-25. Non-SEALED nodes
> (PENDING/BLOCKED_ON_BACKEND) are never archived — they stay in the
> active file so `pick_next` sees them.
>
> **2nd pass, 2026-08-30** (operator-requested, via `/hub-tokens` flag —
> active file had grown back to 30KB, >15KB threshold). Covers the 7
> SEALED nodes dated 2026-08-29 that had accumulated since the 1st pass.

> **3rd pass, 2026-09-03** (operator-requested, `/hub-tokens` flagged the
> active file at 53,917B, ~3.5x the 15KB threshold). Covers every full
> SEALED row still inline as of this pass: 3 nodes dated 2026-09-02
> (`dashboard-visit-list-page`, `dashboard-visit-count-integration`,
> `issue-55-cv-preview-print`), 6 nodes dated 2026-09-01, 5 nodes dated
> 2026-08-31/2026-08-30 (`readme-env-example-cleanup`,
> `rename-repo-refs-cleanup`, `github-pages-base-path-rename`,
> `agent-hub-token-cleanup-20260830`, `issue-7-veeform-component-tests`).
> Every remaining SEALED node in the active file is now a pointer row —
> the only full-content rows left there are the 5 non-SEALED nodes
> (`hub-init` PENDING, 4x `issue-8-jwt-localstorage*` BLOCKED_ON_BACKEND),
> which are never archived.

> **4th pass, 2026-09-06** (operator-requested — "archive đi" — after
> `/hub-tokens` flagged the active file at 20,933B, >15KB threshold, 2
> full SEALED entries not yet archived). Covers the 2 nodes dated
> 2026-09-05: `issue-63-account-settings-page`, `issue-61-forgot-reset-password`.
> Same floor as the 3rd pass — the 5 non-SEALED nodes stay inline, never
> archived.

> **5th pass, 2026-09-18** (operator-requested, via `/sys` sync audit —
> `/hub-tokens`-style check found the active file at 181,183B, far past
> the 15KB threshold). Covers the 25 SEALED nodes dated 2026-09-08
> through 2026-09-16 that had accumulated since the 4th pass:
> `issue-56-public-resume-page`, `remove-report-tokens`, the entire
> 17-node `tailwindcss-*` Bootstrap-to-Tailwind migration series
> (`tailwindcss-setup` through `tailwindcss-post-ship-review-fixes`),
> and 5 `fix-*`/`issue-117-public-slug` nodes. Same floor as prior
> passes — non-SEALED nodes and the newest SEALED node

| `issue-6-api-url-env-vars` | SEALED | [issue #6](https://github.com/datvt243/vue-resume-web/issues/6) — `src/config/api.config.js` hardcode `host === 'localhost' ? ... : 'https://nodejs-resume-api-ts.onrender.com/'`. Thêm `.env.development`/`.env.production` (`VITE_API_URL`), đổi `API` sang đọc `import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL`. GIỮ `subURL = 'api/v1/'` như hằng số riêng (không gộp vào `VITE_API_URL` như ví dụ trong issue) vì `Header.vue` có endpoint `api/me/${email}` KHÔNG đi qua `api/v1/` — gộp sẽ phá endpoint đó (verifier đọc trực tiếp `Header.vue`, xác nhận `getMe()` dùng `${host}api/me/...` và `getFile()` dùng `${host}api/v1/download-pdf`, khớp claim). `.gitignore` đã có sẵn `*.local` che `.env.*.local`, không cần sửa thêm — verifier tự `git check-ignore -v .env.production.local` xác nhận. Verify độc lập bởi verifier: `npm run build` xanh + `onrender.com/` có trong `dist/assets/*.js`, `npm run lint` exit 0, `npm run dev` + curl xác nhận `VITE_API_URL: "http://localhost:3001/"`. **CAVEAT SEAL:** `.env.production` không thực sự là file mới như note ghi ("mới") — file này đã tồn tại trên `main` với nội dung `BASE_URL=/vue-resume-web/`, bị ghi đè hoàn toàn. Không phải regression: `BASE_URL` vẫn còn trong `.env` (base, áp dụng mọi mode) và dù sao cũng là dead code trong `vite.config.ts` (`const base = process.env.BASE_URL || '/'` được gán nhưng không bao giờ dùng — `base:` field hardcode `'/vue-resume-web/'`). Ghi nhận là lỗi ghi chú (evidence-accuracy), không phải lỗi chức năng. **CẬP NHẬT (re-verify pass 2, sau correction):** implementer đã viết lại `.env.production` giữ CẢ 2 dòng (`BASE_URL=/vue-resume-web/` + `VITE_API_URL=...`) — verifier tự `cat`/`git diff main -- .env.production` xác nhận không còn mất dòng nào, `npm run build`/`npm run lint` vẫn xanh, `onrender.com/` vẫn baked đúng trong `dist/assets/*.js`. Caveat coi như đã resolved. Evidence: `evidence/implementer/2026-08-20-issue-6-api-url-env-vars.md`, `evidence/verifier/2026-08-20-issue-6-api-url-env-vars.md`. |

| `register-password-date-currency-validation` | SEALED | Operator: "fix cả 3 bug còn lại đi, gom vào 1" — batches the 3 findings logged as "Noticed, not done" in `i18n-object-fields-remaining`. (1) Register password: backend requires min 12 chars + upper+lower+digit+special (probed via direct API calls with several password variants against `auth/register`), frontend had no complexity rule at all — added a matching Yup schema with per-rule messages + a help text under the field. (2) Date defaults on CREATE were unusable: root-caused to `VeeForm.vue`'s `reset()` hardcoding EVERY field back to `''` (not each field's own `default`) whenever `!doc._id` — harmless for text fields (whose default is already `''`) but fatal for date fields (default is a numeric timestamp), `''` cast to `NaN` by Yup and permanently disabled the submit button. Fixed `reset()` to use `e.default ?? ''`. While fixing, found and fixed a 2nd distinct bug in the SAME area: `startDate`/`endDate` model defaults were both `+new Date()` (identical), which the backend rejects as "endDate phải lớn hơn startDate" once dates round to the same month in monthPicker mode — fixed by making `endDate`'s default +1 month, in both the shared `defaultDateStartEnd()` factory (`model.type.ts`, covers Education/Certificate/Project) and `experience.model.ts` (doesn't use the shared factory). (3) Currency "phải lớn hơn 0" false-positive: root-caused to `FrmCurrency.vue` forcing `value.value = 0` as a placeholder default before the real async document data loads — `0` fails `.positive()` immediately, and if `errorMessage` doesn't clear cleanly before the user notices, it reads as a false "your valid value is wrong" error. Changed the forced placeholder from `0` to `''` (fails the generic `required()` message instead, which is the honest state while data is still loading). Branch `fix/register-password-date-currency-validation` from `main`. Verified end-to-end via real UI clicks+keystrokes (not synthetic events) on the disposable `qa-test-*@example.com` test account: weak password now blocked client-side with the specific rule text; a brand-new Certificate now creates successfully in one real submit ("Created successfully" toast) where it previously could never pass validation. Verifier independently reproduced `npm run build`/`npm run lint` green, read all 5 changed files directly and confirmed each matches the note verbatim, and confirmed the `reset()`/`setValues()` causal chain in `VeeForm.vue` by reading the watcher. **CAVEAT SEAL — evidence-accuracy, not functional:** one of the note's 3 cited password probes, `TestQa12345`, is labeled "12 chars" but is actually 11 (`len('TestQa12345') == 11`) — that specific probe's isolation of "missing special char alone" is not clean since the string is also under the length minimum. Doesn't change the delivered rule: the other 2 probes (12-char+all-classes success, 8-char+all-classes length-only rejection) independently prove the implemented rule is safe (can't let through anything the backend rejects), only possibly stricter than strictly required on an untested edge. Currency fix (bug 3) has NO live reproduction, only code-reasoning — disclosed clearly by the note itself and independently confirmed sound by reading `FrmCurrency.vue` + the real yup v1.4.0 number-transform source (empty string casts to `NaN`, not `0`, so `.positive()`'s false-positive message no longer fires). Evidence: `evidence/implementer/2026-08-22-register-password-date-currency-validation.md`, `evidence/verifier/2026-08-23-register-password-date-currency-validation-seal.md`. |

| `issue-62-dark-mode-body-attr-override` | SEALED | **REGRESSION** on the SEALED node above (new node per LAI-13, that node's status is not touched). Operator manually tested and reported the toggle "chưa work, chưa có light/dark theme". Root cause: `index.html` hardcoded `<body data-bs-theme="dark">`, which shadows `<html>`'s attribute for the entire visible app (closest-ancestor CSS custom-property resolution) — `useTheme.ts` only ever touched `<html>`, so the toggle silently did nothing visible. This shipped past both implementer and verifier on the first pass because neither had a real browser tool that session and settled for unit tests + a curl compile check, which can't catch a conflict with a DIFFERENT file the composable doesn't control. Fix: removed the hardcoded attribute from `index.html`; `useTheme.ts`'s no-saved-value fallback changed `light`→`dark` (preserves the app's existing look, still honors an explicit OS-light preference); added `index.spec.ts` as a permanent regression guard (fails if `data-bs-theme` is ever re-added to `<body>`); updated/added tests in `useTheme.spec.ts` for the new default + OS-dark-vs-no-preference distinction. Branch `fix/issue-62-dark-mode-body-attr-override` from `main`. Verified LIVE this time, not just via unit tests: wrote a throwaway Node script using the transitively-available `node_modules/ws` to drive the already-running debug Chrome (port 9888) over the raw DevTools protocol — cleared localStorage, hard-reloaded, read `document.documentElement`'s attribute + `getComputedStyle(document.body)`, found and real-`.click()`'d the actual toggle button by its title text, re-read state: `html` flipped dark→light and `body` background genuinely changed `rgb(33,37,41)`→`rgb(255,255,255)` on a real click. Verifier independently wrote its OWN separate CDP script (not reusing the implementer's) and reproduced the identical result, plus re-ran `npm run test`/`build`/`lint` (57/57, green, exit 0) and re-read every changed file itself. 57 tests total across 7 files now. Technique logged in `haven/workers/implementer/MEMORY.md` for future UI diffs when no screenshot tool is available. Evidence: `evidence/implementer/2026-08-25-issue-62-dark-mode-body-attr-override.md`, `evidence/verifier/2026-08-25-issue-62-dark-mode-body-attr-override-seal.md`. |

Caught via a real CDP session against the port-9888 debug browser (`Page.navigate` + `Runtime.evaluate` computed-style checks, `doctrine/domains/PROJECT.md`'s recorded technique) — `npm run build`/`lint`/`test` all stayed green through the bug, they can't catch a same-named-class collision. Fixed with an arbitrary-value class (`mt-[1rem]`) that can't collide with any Bootstrap name; re-verified via the same live browser → `marginTop: "16px"`. Also found `.opacity-75` has the same dual-definition collision but both frameworks agree on the value (`opacity: 0.75`), confirmed genuinely harmless (live-checked, not assumed) — **flagging as a general trap for every future node in this migration**: any Tailwind utility class whose bare name collides with one of Bootstrap's own utility names (numeric spacing/margin/padding/opacity/sizing classes 0–5 especially) needs a live-browser value check, not just a build pass, because Bootstrap's `!important` always wins silently. Deliberately left `.text-danger` (ResetPassword) unmigrated — theme-reactive Bootstrap color utility, no Tailwind color/dark-mode token strategy decided yet, logged as follow-up rather than guessed. Evidence: `evidence/implementer/2026-09-12-tailwindcss-auth-pages.md`. Verifier: independent re-derivation (fresh subagent, separate session, never touched the diff) confirmed branch = `feature/tailwindcss-setup` (not `main`/`staging`) via `git branch --show-current`; `git diff --stat -- src/pages/auth/` = exactly the 4 claimed files, 8 insertions/8 deletions, matching the note's class-mapping table line for line, with `<style scoped>` blocks byte-identical (no `<style>` lines in the diff at all). Independently re-derived the Bootstrap/Tailwind `.mt-4` collision claim rather than trusting it: grepped the rebuilt `dist/assets/index-*.css` and found exactly one `.mt-4` rule, Bootstrap's (`margin-top:1.5rem!important`) — Tailwind's own `.mt-4` no longer appears in this build because the source no longer contains the literal class (confirmed via repo-wide `grep -rn '\bmt-4\b' src/`, zero hits, consistent with the fix already being applied). To confirm the collision was real and not imagined, ran an isolated `npx tailwindcss` CLI probe (scratch dir, untracked, this repo's own `tailwind.config.cjs` has no spacing override) against a throwaway HTML file containing literal `mt-4` — confirmed Tailwind's default `.mt-4` compiles to `margin-top:1rem` with no `!important`, which structurally loses to Bootstrap's `!important` rule regardless of CSS source order, proving the collision mechanism the note describes is real, not inferred. Also confirmed in the real build: `.mt-\[1rem\]{margin-top:1rem}` present (the fix, cannot collide with any Bootstrap class name), and `.opacity-75` has exactly two rules — Tailwind's `{opacity:.75}` and Bootstrap's `{opacity:.75!important}` — same computed value, corroborating the note's "harmless, both agree" claim. `CODE_IN_HAVEN` checked clean (`find agent-hub/haven -name "*.vue" -o -name "*.js" -o -name "*.ts" -o -name "*.sh"` → none). | `tailwindcss-post-ship-review-fixes` | SEALED | Operator: real pre-ship QA via `/open-browser-debugger` (real Chrome, remote debugging, operator's own real logged-in account with saved-password autofill) surfaced 2 real bugs + 1 UX request, all fixed. Node 17 of N. **Bug 1**: "page login, header bị lệch" — `Navbar.vue` (unauthenticated header) nests `.container` INSIDE `.navbar`, but node 12's `.navbar{display:flex;justify-content:space-between}` only lays out DIRECT children — with `.container` as the sole child (itself not flex, node 11), brand/toggler/collapse fell back to block/inline flow. Confirmed via CDP `getBoundingClientRect()`: toggler sat right next to the 120px brand at 375px width instead of the far right edge. Real Bootstrap's `_navbar.scss` handles this via a SEPARATE `.navbar > .container` flex rule (its `%container-flex-properties` placeholder) that node 12 never reproduced — didn't affect the AUTHENTICATED header (`Header.vue`'s `v-else`), where `.container` wraps `.navbar` from the outside instead, which is why 5+ prior CDP/harness verification passes never caught it (never exercised the broken path). Fixed: added `.navbar > .container { display:flex; flex-wrap:inherit; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between }`. **Bug 2**: "chỉnh màu input cho phù hợp với dark theme" — Chrome's real saved-password autofill forces `rgb(232,240,254)` (light blue) onto `<input>` via `:-webkit-autofill`, a browser-native override that plain `background-color` can't beat; confirmed via `getComputedStyle` on the operator's actual autofilled field. Fixed with the standard inset-`box-shadow` override trick tinted to `var(--bs-body-bg)` (tracks theme automatically) + `-webkit-text-fill-color` + long `transition-delay`; re-verified live: dark → `rgb(33,37,41)` = `--bs-body-bg` dark, light → `rgb(255,255,255)` = `--bs-body-bg` light. **UX request (not a bug)**: moved `PageLogin.vue`'s "Quên mật khẩu?" link from a separate block below the form into `VeeForm`'s existing `#button` slot (same extension point every CRUD Modal already uses) with `ms-auto self-center`, so it sits on the same row as the Login button. Branch `feature/tailwindcss-setup`. `rm -rf dist && npm run build` → `✓ built in 3.47s`, same pre-existing chunk-size warning only. `npm run lint` → exit 0. `npm run test` → 111/111 passing. Compiled CSS confirmed both fixes present verbatim. **Both bugs re-verified live on the operator's real browser** (CDP port 9888, real logged-in session) after fixing — screenshots + computed-style checks confirm, not just compiled-CSS presence. Disclosed: both bugs are exactly the residual-risk category every CSS node since node 4 flagged ("no live browser") — neither a compiled-CSS check nor the static harness page (used successfully for Modal/Toast/Table in node 15) could have caught a real flex-layout computation bug or browser-native autofill styling; this is why the operator's insistence on real-browser QA before shipping found real issues static verification could not. | `fix-register-login-autofill-autocomplete` | SEALED | Operator report: "page đang ký đang ghi nhớ value của page login" (register page remembers login page's values). Confirms this is NOT a Vue/vee-validate state leak: `VeeForm.vue:59` calls its own `useForm()` per component instance, fully isolated between Login and Register. **Real root cause**: `FrmInput.vue` and `FrmPwd.vue` render `<input>` with no `name` and no `autocomplete` attribute — only `id="email"`/`id="password"`, identical on both pages. Fix: added field-level `autocomplete` hints to `formFields` in `PageLogin.vue` (email→`username`, password→`current-password`) and `PageRegister.vue` (email→`email`, password→`new-password`, repassword→`new-password`) — these fall through to the real `<input>` via `VeeForm.vue`'s existing `v-bind="el"` → `Frm*.vue`'s `useAttrs()` + `v-bind="attrs"` chain, so no `Frm*.vue` component changes were needed. | `fix-header-dropdown-outline-theme` | SEALED | Operator (Vietnamese): "chỉnh lại màu cho dropdown header, light theme" (fix the header dropdown's color for light theme) — follow-up to `fix-ckeditor-light-theme-colors` in the same session. Root cause: `Header.vue:102` hardcodes `Dropdown(:text="mesUser" :style="'outline-light'" ...)`. `.btn-outline-light` (`tailwind.css:331-335`) uses `color/border-color: var(--bs-light)` — `--bs-light` is a Bootstrap THEME-INVARIANT contextual color (fixed `#f8f9fa` always, confirmed: only ever declared once in `tailwind.css`'s root-token block, never redefined under `[data-bs-theme='dark']`, unlike `--bs-tertiary-bg`/`--bs-body-color`/etc. which genuinely differ per theme) — by design meant for permanently-dark surfaces (classic Bootstrap `navbar-dark` pattern), which is what this header used to be. But the header's own background (`header.bg-body-tertiary`, `tailwind.css:991-993`) is theme-REACTIVE (`background-color: var(--bs-tertiary-bg)`), so once the header itself started following the app's light/dark toggle, the hardcoded `outline-light` variant stopped matching that assumption — exact same class of bug as `fix-ckeditor-light-theme-colors` (a fixed/theme-invariant color used where a theme-reactive one was needed), just in a different component. Fix (1 line, `Header.vue:102`): `:style="'outline-light'"` → `:style="theme === 'dark' ? 'outline-light' : 'outline-dark'"` — `theme` was already destructured from `useTheme()` in this file's `<script setup>` (used for the theme-toggle icon), reused here rather than adding new state; `.btn-outline-dark` (`tailwind.css:340-344`) uses `var(--bs-dark)`, the light-theme-appropriate fixed dark color, giving correct contrast against the now-light header in light mode while `outline-light` still applies unchanged in dark mode (no regression to the previously-working dark-theme appearance). Screenshots confirm visually in both themes: "Xin chào, votan.it@gmail.com" and the dropdown caret are now clearly visible in light theme, dark theme unchanged. NOT blocked — implemented the frontend side. **Diff**: (1) `src/routers/index.ts` — renamed the public route's param `/resume/:email` → `/resume/:slug` (comment updated); no separate fallback route needed since the backend resolves the SAME param value as slug-or-email, so an old `/resume/<email>` link still matches this one route pattern unchanged. Confirmed live via a direct unauthenticated-shape PATCH with `slug:""` against the real production API: backend returns `"slug must not be empty"` (Joi `min(3)`, no `.allow('')`) — **disclosed limitation**: there is currently no way to clear a previously-set slug back to empty through this endpoint; logged as a follow-up, not fixed here (backend-only fix, separate repo). `npm run build` → `✓ built in 3.24s`/`3.37s` across 2 builds (1 fix mid-task), only the pre-existing chunk-size warning both times. `npm run lint` → exit 0. `npm run test` → 111/111 passing. **Live-verified end-to-end on the operator's REAL production account** (`votan.it@gmail.com`, via the already-authenticated CDP debug session, port 9888, real backend `https://nodejs-resume-api-ts.onrender.com`): set a temporary test slug → PATCH succeeded (`"Updated successfully"` toast) → persisted after reload → public page `/resume/<slug>` resolved correctly (`cv-name: "Võ Tấn Đạt"`) → old email link `/resume/votan.it@gmail.com` STILL resolves too (backward compat proven, not just assumed). Per the disclosed clear-to-empty limitation, could not revert the test value — **stopped, disclosed to the operator via `AskUserQuestion` immediately** rather than silently leaving polluted real data (`NoSilentFailure`) — operator chose to set a real, meaningful slug (`vo-tan-dat`) instead, done via the app's own new UI, re-verified: backend confirms `slug:"vo-tan-dat"`, public page resolves it correctly. **Second real bug found and disclosed (pre-existing, NOT introduced by this diff, NOT fixed — out of scope)**: a genuine hard page-reload landing directly on `/dashboard/information` (e.g. browser refresh, not an internal SPA link click) shows the ENTIRE form empty — `firstName`, `socialMedia.github`, and the new `slug` field all blank — because `App.vue`'s candidate-hydration fetch (`onMounted`, async) does not block `<RouterView>` from rendering child routes, so nested pages' own one-time `onMounted` seeding can race an in-flight fetch and capture an empty snapshot with no reactive re-seed afterward. Proven with `firstName` (a field this diff never touched) showing the SAME empty-on-hard-reload symptom, isolating this as pre-existing/architectural, not new. Confirmed the NORMAL user flow (land on dashboard root after login, navigate via SPA links) is unaffected — `firstName:"Võ"`/`slug:"vo-tan-dat"` both correct in that flow. Independently re-verified the CORE DESIGN CLAIM by reading the sibling backend repo (`/Users/_david/Workspace/Project/resume/resume-nodejs-api`) directly: `git log --oneline -5 origin/main` confirms `445e57e` really merged/released (v1.5.0); `candidate.validate.ts` confirms `schemaCandidatePatch` declares `slug`, `schemaCandidate` does not; `candidate.controller.ts` confirms `fnUpdate` (PUT) validates against `schemaCandidate` and `fnUpdateFields` (PATCH) validates against `schemaCandidatePatch`; `joi.config.ts`'s `getObject` is a bare `Joi.object(fields)` and `utils/valid.ts`'s `validateSchema` only sets `{ abortEarly: false }` — no `stripUnknown`/`allowUnknown` override anywhere, so Joi's default `unknown(false)` really would reject the whole PUT request if `slug` were sent through it. | `issue-60-duplicate-item` | SEALED | [issue #60](https://github.com/datvt243/resume-vuejs-website/issues/60) — via `/todo`. Real trap found in `VeeForm.vue`'s `watch(() => props.document, ...)`: setting `document._id` falsy triggers an internal `reset()` that wipes whatever was just `setValues()`'d — naively clearing `_id` for a duplicate would silently blank the pre-filled form. | `fix-header-user-menu-style-prop` | SEALED | Regression from `issue-217-app-shell` (SEALED), found during #218 browser check: `Header.vue` passed `style="outline-secondary"` as a static attribute, which Vue parses into a style object, so `Dropdown` rendered `btn btn-[object Object]` (Vue warn "Invalid prop: type check failed for prop style") and the user-menu button had no button styles. A literal `:style` binding is parsed the same way, so the prop was renamed `style` → `variant` in `Dropdown.vue` (Header is its only consumer). Shipped on branch `feature/issue-218-global-components`. **REOPEN by verifier**: note claims Header is the only `Dropdown` consumer, but `src/pages/dashboard/PageReference.vue:98` (`<Dropdown text="" :style="'outline-success border-0'" split>`) still passes `:style` — not migrated to `variant`, no evidence of its rendering. Verdict: `evidence/verifier/2026-10-19-fix-header-user-menu-style-prop-reopen.md`. **Round 2**: `PageReference.vue:98` → `variant="outline-success border-0"`, +1 Dropdown test (9/9), caller list corrected (2 callers, both `variant`). Evidence: `evidence/implementer/2026-10-19-fix-header-user-menu-style-prop-r2.md`. **SEALED by verifier**: `evidence/verifier/2026-10-19-fix-header-user-menu-style-prop-seal-r2.md`. |

Separately, the endpoint only covers the LinkedIn-ZIP half of the issue — PDF text-extraction/parsing remains completely unimplemented anywhere in the backend (`git log --all --grep="pdf" -i` shows only unrelated PDF-export history; `parseLinkedInExport.service.ts` only reads a ZIP via `AdmZip`, no PDF library exists). Frontend re-confirmed to have no import-CV plumbing to extend (`grep -rli "linkedin\|import.*cv\|parse-linkedin\|pdf.*import" src/` → only the pre-existing unrelated `socialMedia.linkedin` URL field). No diff created (would ship a feature that 404s in production, and even then only covers half the issue) — implementer reported `blocked` per `/todo`'s rule, loop stopped immediately, no verifier pass needed. Issue #120 stays OPEN; revisit once backend PR #145 reaches backend `main`, and note PDF import needs a further, separate backend endpoint even then. Evidence: `evidence/implementer/2026-09-26-issue-120-import-cv-pdf-linkedin-recheck.md`. Committed via `chore/issue-120-import-cv-pdf-linkedin-recheck-20260926` off `staging` — same class of hub-only chore branch as the `2026-09-18` precedent of the same name pattern (PR #137); no verifier pass needed (no `src/` diff to verify). |
